10.
Completeness and Dependent Candidate Space

Similar to the simply typed case, our device for proving completeness is some
candidate space. Due to dependent types, of course, we cannot simply port what is in
the simply typed case, because types can embed arbitrary computation. Thus, what we
need here is a little bit more complex, a dependent candidate space. Nevertheless, we
start with two PERs,
\top
and
\bot
, the extrema of the space.

\frac{\forall\alpha,\kappa.\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(d[\kappa])=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(d^{\prime}[\kappa])}{d\approx d^{\prime}\in\top}\par\frac{\forall\alpha,\kappa.\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ty}}_{\alpha}(A[\kappa])=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ty}}_{\alpha}(A^{\prime}[\kappa])}{A\approx A^{\prime}\in\top}\par\frac{\forall\alpha,\kappa.\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(c[\kappa])=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(c^{\prime}[\kappa])}{c\approx c^{\prime}\in\bot}

where
\top\subseteq D^{\textsf{Nf}}\times D^{\textsf{Nf}}
,
\top\subseteq D\times D
(
\top
is
overloaded because one is meant for values and the other is meant for types) and

\bot\subseteq D^{\textsf{Ne}}\times D^{\textsf{Ne}}
. It is easy to show that they are both PERs.

Now we need one PER for each semantic type, plus types represented by neutral
terms. We begin with
N
, which is the easiest case:

\frac{}{\texttt{ze}\approx\texttt{ze}\in Nat}\par\frac{a\approx b\in Nat}{\texttt{su}(a)\approx\texttt{su}(b)\in Nat}\par\frac{c\approx c^{\prime}\in\bot}{\uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(c)\approx\uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(c^{\prime})\in Nat}

Here we use
Nat
to relate the semantic values that are considered equivalent natural
numbers.
We can prove that
Nat
is a PER by consider all cases and use the fact that
\bot
is
also a PER.

The next one is values related by a neutral type. We virtually can not do
anything but to related them by
\bot
:

\frac{c\approx c^{\prime}\in\bot}{\uparrow^{A}(c)\approx\uparrow^{A^{\prime}}(c^{\prime})\in Neu}

Here the neutral type is represented by a domain neutral value
C
. Effectively,

\uparrow^{A}(c)
and
\uparrow^{A^{\prime}}(c^{\prime})
are related, if
c
and
c^{\prime}
are also
related by
\bot
.
A
and
A^{\prime}
have no particular relation. It is immediate that

Neu
is a PER.

For the remaining PERs for universes,
\blacksquare
and
Pi
, we define them
simultaneously by using inductive-recursive definitions:

\frac{C\approx C^{\prime}\in\bot}{\uparrow^{A}(C)\approx\uparrow^{A^{\prime}}(C^{\prime})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}\par\frac{}{\textsf{N}\approx\textsf{N}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}\par\frac{j<i}{\textsf{U}_{j}\approx\textsf{U}_{j}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}\par\frac{\forall\kappa.A[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}{\blacksquare A\approx\blacksquare A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}\par\frac{\forall\kappa.A[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}\quad\forall\kappa,a\approx a^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A[\kappa]).\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,a))\approx\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}{Pi(A,x.T,\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx Pi(A^{\prime},x.T^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

In the
Pi
case, we use
\textbf{El}_{i}
to compute a relation from
A
, which relates

a
and
a^{\prime}
and is defined below. By letting
\textbf{El}_{i}(A)
is defined recursively on
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
,
we only need to consider the values quantified by
\mathcal{U}_{i}
.

\displaystyle\textbf{El}_{i}(\uparrow^{A}(C))

\displaystyle:=Neu

\displaystyle\textbf{El}_{i}(\textsf{N})

\displaystyle:=Nat

\displaystyle\textbf{El}_{i}(\textsf{U}_{j})

\displaystyle:=\mathcal{U}_{j}

\displaystyle\textbf{El}_{i}(\blacksquare A)

\displaystyle:=\{(a,b)\;|\;\forall\kappa,n.\texttt{unbox}\cdot(n,a[\kappa])\approx\texttt{unbox}\cdot(n,b[\kappa])\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A[\kappa;\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}])\}

\displaystyle\textbf{El}_{i}(Pi(A,x.T,\overrightarrow{\rho}))

\displaystyle:=\{(a,b)\;|\;\forall\kappa,a^{\prime}\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A[\kappa]).a[\kappa]\cdot a^{\prime}\approx b[\kappa]\cdot b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime})))\}

In the next section, we verify properties of the definitions we just had.

10.1.
Properties of Candidate Space

Lemma 10.1.

If
d\approx d^{\prime}\in\top
, then
d[\kappa]\approx d^{\prime}[\kappa]\in\top
.

Lemma 10.2.

If
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\top
, then
A[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime}[\kappa]\in\top
.

Lemma 10.3.

If
c\approx c^{\prime}\in\bot
, then
c[\kappa]\approx c^{\prime}[\kappa]\in\bot
.

Proof.

Immediate due to universal quantification.
∎

The following two lemmas are proved mutually:

Lemma 10.4.

If
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
, then
a\approx b\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A)
and
a\approx b\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime})
are equivalent.

Lemma 10.5.

\mathcal{U}_{i}
and if
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
,
\textbf{El}_{i}(A)
are PERs.

Proof of
Lemma

10.4
.

We do induction on
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
. We omit the easy cases.

Case

A=\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime}
,
A^{\prime}=\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime\prime}
,

\frac{\forall\kappa.A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}{\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime}\approx\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

(assumption)

\displaystyle\forall\kappa,n.\texttt{unbox}\cdot(n,a[\kappa])\approx\texttt{unbox}\cdot(n,b[\kappa])\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}])

\displaystyle\text{assume }\kappa,n,

\displaystyle A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}]\approx A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

(by IH)

\displaystyle\texttt{unbox}\cdot(n,a[\kappa])\approx\texttt{unbox}\cdot(n,b[\kappa])\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}])

(by abstraction)

\displaystyle a\approx b\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime\prime})

Case

A=Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime})
,
A^{\prime}=Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime})

\frac{\forall\kappa.A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}\quad\forall\kappa,a^{\prime}\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]).\llbracket T^{\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime}))\approx\llbracket T^{\prime\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,b^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}{Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime})\approx Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

(by assumption)

\displaystyle\forall\kappa,a^{\prime}\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]).a[\kappa]\cdot a^{\prime}\approx b[\kappa]\cdot b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket T^{\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime})))

\displaystyle\text{assume }\kappa,a^{\prime}\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa])

\displaystyle A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

(by IH)

\displaystyle a^{\prime}\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa])

\displaystyle a[\kappa]\cdot a^{\prime}\approx b[\kappa]\cdot b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket T^{\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime})))

\displaystyle\llbracket T^{\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime}))\approx\llbracket T^{\prime\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,b^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

(by IH)

\displaystyle a[\kappa]\cdot a^{\prime}\approx b[\kappa]\cdot b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket T^{\prime\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,b^{\prime})))

(by abstraction)

\displaystyle a\approx b\in\textbf{El}_{i}(Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime}))

The other direction is proved symmetrically.
∎

Proof of
Lemma

10.5
.

Again we only consider interesting cases. First consider symmetricity. We proceed by
induction on
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
:

Case

A=Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime})
,
A^{\prime}=Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime})
,

\frac{\forall\kappa.A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}\quad\forall\kappa,a^{\prime}\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]).\llbracket T^{\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime}))\approx\llbracket T^{\prime\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,b^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}{Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime})\approx Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

\displaystyle\text{assume }\kappa,a^{\prime}\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa])

\displaystyle A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

(by
Lemma

10.4
)

\displaystyle a^{\prime}\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa])

\displaystyle\llbracket T^{\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime}))\approx\llbracket T^{\prime\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,b^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

(by IH)

\displaystyle\llbracket T^{\prime\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,b^{\prime}))\approx\llbracket T^{\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

\displaystyle Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime})\approx Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

Next we consider symmetry of
\textbf{El}_{i}(Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}))
.

(by assumption)

\displaystyle\forall\kappa,a^{\prime}\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]).a[\kappa]\cdot a^{\prime}\approx b[\kappa]\cdot b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket T^{\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime})))

\displaystyle\text{assume }\kappa,b^{\prime}\approx a^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa])

(by IH)

\displaystyle a^{\prime}\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa])

\displaystyle a[\kappa]\cdot a^{\prime}\approx b[\kappa]\cdot b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket T^{\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime})))

(by IH)

\displaystyle b[\kappa]\cdot b^{\prime}\approx a[\kappa]\cdot a^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket T^{\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime})))

(by
Lemma

10.4
)

\displaystyle b[\kappa]\cdot b^{\prime}\approx a[\kappa]\cdot a^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket T^{\prime\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,b^{\prime})))

(by abstraction)

\displaystyle b\approx a\in\textbf{El}_{i}(Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime}))

(by
Lemma

10.4
)

\displaystyle b\approx a\in\textbf{El}_{i}(Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}))

Now we consider transitivity. We should show

•

If
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
and
A^{\prime}\approx A^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
, then
A\approx A^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
.

•

If
a\approx a^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A)
and
a^{\prime}\approx a^{\prime\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A)
, then
a\approx a^{\prime\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A)
.

We do induction on
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
and invert
A^{\prime}\approx A^{\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
. We only consider the the interesting
cases.

Case

A=Pi(A_{1},x.T_{1},\overrightarrow{\rho}_{1})
,
A^{\prime}=Pi(A_{2},x.T_{2},\overrightarrow{\rho}_{2})
,
A^{\prime\prime}=Pi(A_{3},x.T_{3},\overrightarrow{\rho}_{3})

\frac{\forall\kappa.A_{1}[\kappa]\approx A_{2}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}\quad\forall\kappa,b\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A_{1}[\kappa]).\llbracket T_{1}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}_{1}[\kappa],x,b))\approx\llbracket T_{1}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}_{2}[\kappa],x,b^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}{Pi(A_{1},x.T_{1},\overrightarrow{\rho}_{1})\approx Pi(A_{2},x.T_{2},\overrightarrow{\rho}_{2})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}\par\frac{\forall\kappa.A_{2}[\kappa]\approx A_{3}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}\quad\forall\kappa,b\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A_{2}[\kappa]).\llbracket T_{2}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}_{2}[\kappa],x,b))\approx\llbracket T_{3}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}_{3}[\kappa],x,b^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}{Pi(A_{2},x.T_{2},\overrightarrow{\rho}_{2})\approx Pi(A_{3},x.T_{3},\overrightarrow{\rho}_{3})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

\displaystyle\text{assume }\kappa,b\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A_{1}[\kappa])

\displaystyle\llbracket T_{1}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}_{1}[\kappa],x,b))\approx\llbracket T_{1}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}_{2}[\kappa],x,b^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

(by
Lemma

10.4
)

\displaystyle b\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A_{2}[\kappa])

(by IH)

\displaystyle b^{\prime}\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A_{2}[\kappa])

\displaystyle\llbracket T_{2}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}_{2}[\kappa],x,b^{\prime}))\approx\llbracket T_{3}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}_{3}[\kappa],x,b^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

(by IH)

\displaystyle\llbracket T_{1}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}_{1}[\kappa],x,b))\approx\llbracket T_{3}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}_{3}[\kappa],x,b^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

(by abstraction)

\displaystyle Pi(A_{1},x.T_{1},\overrightarrow{\rho}_{1})\approx Pi(A_{3},x.T_{3},\overrightarrow{\rho}_{3})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

We move on to the transitivity of
Pi(A_{1},x.T_{1},\overrightarrow{\rho}_{1})
.

\displaystyle\text{assume }\kappa,b\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A_{1}[\kappa])

\displaystyle a[\kappa]\cdot b\approx a^{\prime}[\kappa]\cdot b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket T_{1}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}_{1}[\kappa],x,b)))

(by IH)

\displaystyle b^{\prime}\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A_{1}[\kappa])

\displaystyle a^{\prime}[\kappa]\cdot b^{\prime}\approx a^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]\cdot b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket T_{1}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}_{1}[\kappa],x,b^{\prime})))

(by
Lemma

10.4
)

\displaystyle a^{\prime}[\kappa]\cdot b^{\prime}\approx a^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]\cdot b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket T_{1}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}_{1}[\kappa],x,b)))

(by IH)

\displaystyle a[\kappa]\cdot b\approx a^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]\cdot b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket T_{1}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}_{1}[\kappa],x,b)))

\displaystyle a\approx a^{\prime\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(Pi(A_{1},x.T_{1},\overrightarrow{\rho}))

∎

The previous two lemmas justify that
\textbf{El}_{i}(A)
is define if
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
or

A^{\prime}\approx A\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
holds. Thus, notationally we can write
A\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
to express
the well-defined condition for
\textbf{El}_{i}(A)
.

The following two lemmas are needed and can be proved just like in the simply typed
case.

Lemma 10.6.

\mathcal{O}({\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})},{n})=\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\rho}},{\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\sigma}},{n})})

Lemma 10.7.

\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\mid{n}=\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\mid{n}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}\mid{\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\sigma}},{n})})

Lemma 10.8 (Monotonicity).

•

If
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
, then
A[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
.

•

If
a\approx b\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A)
, then
a[\kappa]\approx b[\kappa]\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A[\kappa])
.

Proof.

Induction on
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
. All cases are discharged due to the universal
quantification over UMoTs.
∎

Next, we establish that the PER model is cumulative. We first show that the model is
cumulative by one step.

Lemma 10.9 (Cumulativity and lowering).

If
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
,

•

then
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i+1}
;

•

if
a\approx b\in\textbf{El}_{i+1}(A)
, then
a\approx b\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A)
.

•

if
a\approx b\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A)
, then
a\approx b\in\textbf{El}_{i+1}(A)
.

Proof.

We proceed by induction on
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
. Most cases are
straightforward. However, in the cases of
Pi
and
\Lambda
, we are given

a^{\prime}\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i+1}(A^{\prime})
for some
A^{\prime}
and need to obtain

a^{\prime}\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime})
to invoke the premise and use IH. This therefore
requires us to prove a
lowering
lemma simultaneously, which lowers the
universe level of
El
. This allows us to conclude the goal.
∎

Lemma 10.10 (Cumulativity).

If
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
and
i\leq k
,

•

then
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{k}
;

•

if
a\approx b\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A)
, then
a\approx b\in\textbf{El}_{k}(A)
.

Proof.

i\leq k
means
k=i+n
for some
n
. We do induction on
n
.
∎

This lemma shows that
\mathcal{U}_{i}
indeed models the unvierses cumulatively and thus further
justifies that we can take the limit of
\mathcal{U}_{i}
and
\textbf{El}_{i}
. We write
\mathcal{U}
for

\mathcal{U}_{\infty}
and
El
for
\textbf{El}_{\infty}
. All previous lemmas about
\mathcal{U}_{i}
and
\textbf{El}_{i}

hold for
\mathcal{U}
and
El
. By construction, we have

Lemma 10.11 (subsumption of limits).

•

If
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
, then
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}
.

•

If
a\approx b\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A)
, then
a\approx b\in\textbf{El}(A)
.

Now we have
\mathcal{U}
to relate two types and
El
to relate two values. We then further
extend this relation to context stack as follows:

\frac{}{\vDash\epsilon;\cdot\approx\epsilon;\cdot}\par\frac{\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\approx\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}}{\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\cdot\approx\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime};\cdot}\par\frac{\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\approx\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime};\Gamma^{\prime}\quad\forall\overrightarrow{\rho}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\rrbracket.\llbracket T\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{U}}{\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:T)\approx\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime};(\Gamma^{\prime},x:T^{\prime})}

By induction-recursion, we define the following relation relating two evaluation environments:

\displaystyle\llbracket\epsilon;\cdot\rrbracket

\displaystyle:=\{(\overrightarrow{\rho},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\}

\displaystyle\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\cdot\rrbracket

\displaystyle:=\{(\overrightarrow{\rho},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\;|\;\overrightarrow{\rho}\mid{1}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\mid{1}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\rrbracket\text{ and }k=k^{\prime}\text{ where }(k,\_):=\overrightarrow{\rho}(0)\text{ and }(k^{\prime},\_):=\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}(0)\}

\displaystyle\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:T)\rrbracket

\displaystyle:=\{(\overrightarrow{\rho},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\;|\;\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x)\approx\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime},x)\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\rrbracket\text{ and }\rho(x)\approx\rho^{\prime}(x)\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x)))

\displaystyle\qquad\text{ where }(\_,\rho):=\overrightarrow{\rho}(0)\text{ and }(\_,\rho^{\prime}):=\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}(0)\}

where
\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\rrbracket
is defined by recursion on
\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\approx\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}
.

Interpretation of context stacks remains closed under modal transformations:

Lemma 10.12.

Given
\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\approx\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}
, if

\overrightarrow{\rho}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\rrbracket
, then

\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa]\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}[\kappa]\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\rrbracket
.

Proof.

Immediate by induction on
\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\approx\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}
and
Lemma

10.8
.
∎

Lemma 10.13.

•

\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\approx\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}
is a PER.

•

If
\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\approx\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}
and

\overrightarrow{\rho}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\rrbracket
, then

\overrightarrow{\rho}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}\rrbracket
.

Proof.

Immediate by induction on
\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\approx\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}
and
Lemma

10.4
.
∎

Lemma 10.14.

If
\overrightarrow{\rho}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\rrbracket
, then
\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\rho}},{n})=\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}},{n})
.

Lemma 10.15.

If
\overrightarrow{\rho}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\rrbracket
, then
\overrightarrow{\rho}\mid{n}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\mid{n}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\mid{n}\rrbracket
.

We then can define the semantic judgments:

Definition 10.16.

We define semantic typing and semantic typing equivalence as follows:

\displaystyle\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}

\displaystyle:=\ \vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\approx\overrightarrow{\Gamma}

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash t\approx t^{\prime}:T

\displaystyle:=\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\text{ and }\forall\overrightarrow{\rho}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\rrbracket.\llbracket T\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx\llbracket T\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{U}\text{ and }\llbracket t\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx\llbracket t^{\prime}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}))

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}

\displaystyle:=\ \vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\text{ and }\vDash\overrightarrow{\Delta}\text{ and }\forall\overrightarrow{\rho}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\rrbracket.\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Delta}\rrbracket

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash t:T

\displaystyle:=\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash t\approx t:T

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}

\displaystyle:=\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}

Similar to the simply typed case, naturality equalities are also present in the
semantic judgments.

10.2.
Realizability

Similar to the simply typed case, we need realizability to convert the PER model to
the
\top
PER so that it serves as the final step of the completeness proof.

Lemma 10.17.

z\approx z\in\bot

Proof.

Immediate by definition.
∎

Theorem 10.18 (realizability).

Given
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
,

•

If
c\approx c^{\prime}\in\bot
, then
\uparrow^{A}(c)\approx\uparrow^{A^{\prime}}(c^{\prime})\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A)
;

•

If
a\approx a^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A)
, then
\downarrow^{A}(a)\approx\downarrow^{A^{\prime}}(a^{\prime})\in\top
;

•

A\approx A^{\prime}\in\top
.

Proof.

We do induction on
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
,

Case

A=\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime}}(C)
,
A^{\prime}=\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime\prime}}(C^{\prime})
,

\frac{C\approx C^{\prime}\in\bot}{\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime}}(C)\approx\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime\prime}}(C^{\prime})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

Immediate because the first statement is just identity, and the second and the
third statements
follow immediately by expanding definition.

Case

A=A^{\prime}=\textsf{N}
,

\frac{}{\textsf{N}\approx\textsf{N}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

Only need to check the second statement. We further do induction on
a\approx a^{\prime}\in Nat
and the conclusion follows by expanding the definitions.

Case

A=A^{\prime}=\textsf{U}_{j}
,

\frac{j<i}{\textsf{U}_{j}\approx\textsf{U}_{j}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

All statements are immediate(apply IH when necessary).

Case

A=\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime}
,
A^{\prime}=\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime\prime}
,

\frac{\forall\kappa.A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}{\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime}\approx\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

Subcase

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{1}:

\displaystyle\ c\approx c^{\prime}\in\bot

\displaystyle\ \text{assume }\kappa,n,\alpha,\kappa^{\prime}

(by
H_{1}
)

\displaystyle\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(c[\kappa\circ(\kappa^{\prime}\mid{n})])=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(c^{\prime}[\kappa\circ(\kappa^{\prime}\mid{n})])

(by congruence)

\displaystyle\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(\texttt{unbox}(\mathcal{O}({\kappa^{\prime}},{n}),c[\kappa\circ(\kappa^{\prime}\mid{n})]))=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(\texttt{unbox}(\mathcal{O}({\kappa^{\prime}},{n}),c^{\prime}[\kappa\circ(\kappa^{\prime}\mid{n})]))

(by definition)

\displaystyle\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(\texttt{unbox}(n,c[\kappa])[\kappa^{\prime}])=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(\texttt{unbox}(n,c^{\prime}[\kappa])[\kappa^{\prime}])

(by abstraction)

\displaystyle\ \texttt{unbox}(n,c[\kappa])\approx\texttt{unbox}(n,c^{\prime}[\kappa])\in\bot

(by IH)

\displaystyle\ \uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}]}(\texttt{unbox}(n,c[\kappa]))\approx\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{k}\!\!{}]}(\texttt{unbox}(n,c^{\prime}[\kappa]))\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}])

(by definition)

\displaystyle\ \texttt{unbox}\cdot(n,\uparrow^{\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime}}(c)[\kappa])\approx\texttt{unbox}\cdot(n,\uparrow^{\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime\prime}}(c^{\prime})[\kappa])\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}])

(by abstraction)

\displaystyle\ \uparrow^{\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime}}(c)\approx\uparrow^{\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime\prime}}(c^{\prime})\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime})

Subcase

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{2}:

\displaystyle\ a\approx a^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime})

\displaystyle\ \text{assume }\alpha,\kappa

(by
H_{2}
)

\displaystyle\ \texttt{unbox}\cdot(1,a[\kappa])\approx\texttt{unbox}\cdot(1,a^{\prime}[\kappa])\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{1}\!\!{}])

(by IH)

\displaystyle H_{3}:

\displaystyle\ \downarrow^{A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{1}\!\!{}]}(\texttt{unbox}\cdot(1,a[\kappa]))\approx\downarrow^{A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{1}\!\!{}]}(\texttt{unbox}\cdot(1,a^{\prime}[\kappa]))\in\top

(by
H_{3}
)

\displaystyle\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(\downarrow^{A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{1}\!\!{}]}(\texttt{unbox}\cdot(1,a[\kappa])))=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(\downarrow^{A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{1}\!\!{}]}(\texttt{unbox}\cdot(1,a^{\prime}[\kappa])))

(by congruence and definition)

\displaystyle\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(\downarrow^{\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(a[\kappa]))=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(\downarrow^{\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(a^{\prime}[\kappa]))

(by definition)

\displaystyle\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(\downarrow^{\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime}}(a)[\kappa])=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(\downarrow^{\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime\prime}}(a^{\prime})[\kappa])

(by abstraction)

\displaystyle\ \downarrow^{\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime}}(a)\approx\downarrow^{\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime}}(a^{\prime})\in\top

Subcase

\displaystyle\ \text{assume }\alpha,\kappa

\displaystyle\ A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{1}\!\!{}]\approx A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{1}\!\!{}]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

(by IH)

\displaystyle H_{4}:

\displaystyle\ A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{1}\!\!{}]\approx A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{1}\!\!{}]\in\top

(by
H_{4}
)

\displaystyle\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ty}}_{\alpha}(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{1}\!\!{}])=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ty}}_{\alpha}(A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa;\Uparrow^{1}\!\!{}])

(by congruence)

\displaystyle\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ty}}_{\alpha}(\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa])=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ty}}_{\alpha}(\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa])

\displaystyle\ \blacksquare A^{\prime\prime}\approx\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime\prime}\in\top

Case

A=Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime})
,
A^{\prime}=Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime})
,

\frac{\forall\kappa.A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}\quad\forall\kappa,a^{\prime}\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]).\llbracket T^{\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime}))\approx\llbracket T^{\prime\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,b^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}{Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime})\approx Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

Subcase

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{5}:

\displaystyle\ c\approx c^{\prime}\in\bot

\displaystyle\ \text{assume }\kappa,b\approx b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]),\alpha,\kappa^{\prime}

(by
H_{5}
)

\displaystyle\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(c[\kappa\circ\kappa^{\prime}])=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(c^{\prime}[\kappa\circ\kappa^{\prime}])

(by IH)

\displaystyle H_{6}:

\displaystyle\ \downarrow^{A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(b)\approx\downarrow^{A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(b^{\prime})\in\top

(by
H_{6}
)

\displaystyle\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(\downarrow^{A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(b)[\kappa^{\prime}])=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(\downarrow^{A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(b^{\prime})[\kappa^{\prime}])

(by congruence)

\displaystyle\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(c[\kappa][\kappa^{\prime}])\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(\downarrow^{A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(b)[\kappa^{\prime}])=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(c^{\prime}[\kappa][\kappa^{\prime}])\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(\downarrow^{A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(b^{\prime})[\kappa^{\prime}])

(by definition)

\displaystyle\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}((c[\kappa]\ \downarrow^{A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(b))[\kappa^{\prime}])=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}((c^{\prime}[\kappa]\ \downarrow^{A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(b^{\prime}))[\kappa^{\prime}])

(by abstraction)

\displaystyle\ c[\kappa]\ \downarrow^{A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(b)\approx c^{\prime}[\kappa]\ \downarrow^{A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(b^{\prime})\in\bot

(by definition)

\displaystyle\ \uparrow^{Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime})}(c)[\kappa]\cdot b\approx\uparrow^{Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime})}(c^{\prime})[\kappa]\cdot b^{\prime}\in\bot

(by IH)

\displaystyle\ \uparrow^{Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime})}(c)[\kappa]\cdot b\approx\uparrow^{Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime})}(c^{\prime})[\kappa]\cdot b^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket T^{\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,b)))

\displaystyle\ \uparrow^{Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime})}(c)\approx\uparrow^{Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime})}(c^{\prime})\in\textbf{El}_{i}(Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}))

Subcase

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{7}:

\displaystyle\ a\approx a^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}))

\displaystyle\ \text{assume }\alpha,\kappa

(for some
z
, by
Lemma

10.17
)

\displaystyle\ z\approx z\in\bot

(by IH)

\displaystyle\ \uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(z)\approx\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(z)\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa])

(by
H_{7}
, where
B:=\llbracket T^{\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(z)))
)

\displaystyle\ a[\kappa]\cdot\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(z)\approx a^{\prime}[\kappa]\cdot\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(z)\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket T^{\prime\prime}\rrbracket(B))

(by IH, where
B^{\prime}:=\llbracket T^{\prime\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(z)))
)

\displaystyle H_{8}:

\displaystyle\ \downarrow^{B}(a[\kappa]\cdot\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(z))\approx\downarrow^{B^{\prime}}(a^{\prime}[\kappa]\cdot\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(z))\in\top

(by
H_{8}
)

\displaystyle\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha[z\mapsto x]}(\downarrow^{B}(a[\kappa]\cdot\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(z)))=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha[z\mapsto x]}(\downarrow^{B^{\prime}}(a^{\prime}[\kappa]\cdot\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(z)))

(by congruence)

\displaystyle\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(\downarrow^{Pi(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa],x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}[\kappa])}(a[\kappa]))=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(\downarrow^{Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa],x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa])}(a^{\prime}[\kappa]))

(by definition)

\displaystyle\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(\downarrow^{Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime})}(a)[\kappa])=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(\downarrow^{Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime})}(a^{\prime})[\kappa])

(by abstraction)

\displaystyle\ \downarrow^{Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime})}(a)\approx\downarrow^{Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime})}(a^{\prime})\in\top

Subcase

\displaystyle\ \text{assume }\alpha,\kappa

(for some
z
, by
Lemma

10.17
)

\displaystyle\ z\approx z\in\bot

(by IH)

\displaystyle\ \uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(z)\approx\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(z)\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa])

(where
B:=\llbracket T^{\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]}(z)))
and

\displaystyle\ B\approx B^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

(by IH)

\displaystyle\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ty}}_{{\alpha[z\mapsto x]}}(B)=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ty}}_{\alpha[z\mapsto x]}(B^{\prime})

(by congruence)

\displaystyle\ \textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ty}}_{\alpha}(\Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime})[\kappa])=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ty}}_{\alpha}(\Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime})[\kappa])

(by abstraction)

\displaystyle\ \Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime})\approx\Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime})\in\top

∎

10.3.
Completeness

10.3.1.
Well-formed Context Stacks

Lemma 10.19.

\frac{}{\vDash\epsilon;\cdot\approx\epsilon;\cdot}\par\frac{\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\approx\overrightarrow{\Delta}}{\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\cdot\approx\overrightarrow{\Delta};\cdot}\par\frac{\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\approx\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash T\approx T^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}}{\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:T)\approx\overrightarrow{\Delta};(\Delta,x:T^{\prime})}

Proof.

Immediate.
∎

10.3.2.
Conversions

Lemma 10.20.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash t\approx t^{\prime}:T\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash T\approx T^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash t\approx t^{\prime}:T^{\prime}}

Proof.

Assuming
\overrightarrow{\rho}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\rrbracket
, we know
\llbracket t\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx\llbracket t^{\prime}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}))
and
\llbracket T\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\in\mathcal{U}
. We obtain
\llbracket t\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx\llbracket t^{\prime}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}))
by
Lemma

10.4
.

Naturality equalities are direct consequences.
∎

Lemma 10.21.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\quad\vDash\overrightarrow{\Delta}\approx\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}}

Proof.

Immediate by PER and
Lemma

10.13
.
∎

All substitution related rules are immediate as they do essentially the same thing as the simply
typed case.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vDash t\approx t^{\prime}:T\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx t^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}]:T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]}

\frac{\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{I}\approx\overrightarrow{I}:\overrightarrow{\Gamma}}\par\frac{\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:T)}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:T)\vDash\textsf{wk}_{x}\approx\textsf{wk}_{x}:\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma}\par\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime};\Gamma\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime};\Gamma\vDash T:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash t\approx t^{\prime}:T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma},t/x\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma},t^{\prime}/x:\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime};(\Gamma,x:T)}\par\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\quad\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}\quad|\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}|=n}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma};\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime};\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};\cdot}\par\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime\prime}\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\delta}\approx\overrightarrow{\delta}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime\prime}}

We rules are immediate by following the definitions.

10.3.5.
\Pi

Lemma 10.29.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime};\Gamma\vDash S\approx S^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime};(\Gamma,x:S)\vDash T\approx T^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime};\Gamma\vDash\Pi(x:S).T\approx\Pi(x:S^{\prime}).T^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}}

Proof.

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{1}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime};\Gamma\vDash S\approx S^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{2}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime};(\Gamma,x:S)\vDash T\approx T^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{3}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\rho}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime};\Gamma\rrbracket

\displaystyle\ \text{assume }\kappa

(by
H_{1}
)

\displaystyle\ \llbracket S\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx\llbracket S\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

(by
Lemma

10.8
, then abstraction)

\displaystyle\ \forall\kappa.\llbracket S\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})[\kappa]\approx\llbracket S\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

\displaystyle\ \text{assume }\kappa,a\approx a^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket S\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})[\kappa])

(by naturality equality of
S
)

\displaystyle\ a\approx a^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket S\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa]))

(by
H_{3}
and
Lemma

10.12
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa]\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}[\kappa]\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime};\Gamma\rrbracket

(by definition)

\displaystyle H_{4}:

\displaystyle\ \texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,a)\approx\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime})\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime};(\Gamma,x:S)\rrbracket

(by
H_{2}
and
H_{4}
)

\displaystyle\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,a))\approx\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

(by abstraction)

\displaystyle\ Pi(\llbracket S\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}),x.T,\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx Pi(\llbracket S^{\prime}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}),x.T^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

Naturality equalities are immediate.
∎

Lemma 10.30.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:S)\vDash t\approx t^{\prime}:T}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash\lambda x.t\approx\lambda x.t^{\prime}:\Pi(x:S).T}

Proof.

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{1}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:S)\vDash t\approx t^{\prime}:T

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{2}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\rho}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime};\Gamma\rrbracket

\displaystyle\ \text{assume }\kappa,a\approx a^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket S\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})[\kappa])

(by
H_{2}
and
Lemma

10.12
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa]\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}[\kappa]\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime};\Gamma\rrbracket

(by definition)

\displaystyle H_{3}:

\displaystyle\ \texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,a)\approx\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime})\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime};(\Gamma,x:S)\rrbracket

(by
H_{1}
and
H_{3}
)

\displaystyle\ \llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,a))\approx\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{U}

(by inverting
H_{1}
and
Lemma

10.8
)

\displaystyle\ \forall\kappa.\llbracket S\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})[\kappa]\approx\llbracket S\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}

(first goal, by abstraction)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash\Pi(x:S).T

(by
H_{1}
)

\displaystyle\ \llbracket t\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,a))\approx\llbracket t^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime}))\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,a)))

(by abstraction)

\displaystyle\ \llbracket t\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx\llbracket t^{\prime}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket\Pi(x:S).T\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}))

Naturality equalities are immediate.
∎

Lemma 10.31.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash t\approx t^{\prime}:\Pi(x:S).T\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash s\approx s^{\prime}:S}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash t\ s\approx t^{\prime}\ s^{\prime}:T[\overrightarrow{I},x:s]}

Proof.

Follow definition. Naturality equalities hold for the same reason given in the
simply typed case.
∎

Lemma 10.32.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:S)\vDash t:T\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash s:S}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash(\lambda x.t)\ s\approx t[\overrightarrow{I},s/x]:T[\overrightarrow{I},s/x]}

Proof.

Following the definition.
\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:S)\vDash t:T
will give us
the desired relation.
∎

Lemma 10.33.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash t:\Pi(x:S).T}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash t\approx\lambda x.t[\textsf{wk}_{x}]\ x:\Pi(x:S).T}

Proof.

t
and
\lambda x.t[p(\overrightarrow{I},x)]\ x
are the same modulo evaluation environments
after evaluation, and they can be related just by the premise.
∎

Lemma 10.34.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta\quad\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta\vDash S:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\quad\overrightarrow{\Delta};(\Delta,x:S)\vDash T:\texttt{Ty}_{i}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash\Pi(x:S).T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\Pi(x:S[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]).(T[q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x)]):\texttt{Ty}_{i}}

Proof.

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{1}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{2}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta\vDash S:\texttt{Ty}_{i}

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{3}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta};(\Delta,x:S)\vDash T:\texttt{Ty}_{i}

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{4}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\rho}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\rrbracket

We evaluate both sides:

\displaystyle\llbracket\Pi(x:S).T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})

\displaystyle=Pi(\llbracket S\rrbracket(\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})),x.T,(\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})))

\displaystyle\llbracket{\Pi(x:S[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]).(T[q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x)])}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})

\displaystyle=Pi(\llbracket S\rrbracket(\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})),x.T[q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x)],\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})

We want to related them by
\mathcal{U}_{i}
. Assuming
\kappa
,
a\approx a^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket S\rrbracket(\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}))[\kappa])
,

(by naturality equality)

\displaystyle\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})[\kappa],x,a))

\displaystyle=\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa]),x,a))

\displaystyle\llbracket T\rrbracket(\llbracket q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x)\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime})))

\displaystyle=\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}[\kappa]),x,a^{\prime}))

They only differ by evaluation environments, so we can conclude the goal by applying

Lemma

10.12
and then abstraction. Moreover, natural equalities
hold because of natural equalities of
T
.
∎

Lemma 10.35.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta\quad\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta,x:S\vDash t:T}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash\lambda x.t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\lambda x.(t[q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x)]):\Pi(x:S).T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]}

Proof.

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{1}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{2}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta,x:S\vDash t:T

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{3}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\rho}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\rrbracket

We evaluate both sides:

\displaystyle\llbracket\lambda x.t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})

\displaystyle=\Lambda(x.t,\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}))

\displaystyle\llbracket\lambda x.(t[q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x)])\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})

\displaystyle=\Lambda(x.t[q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x)],\overrightarrow{\rho})

Assuming
\kappa
,
a\approx a^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\llbracket S\rrbracket(\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}))[\kappa])
,
we applying both sides to them, and then from
H_{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\llbracket t\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})[\kappa],x,a))\approx\llbracket t\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})[\kappa],x,a^{\prime}))\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})[\kappa],x,a)))

By abstraction, we have

\displaystyle\llbracket\lambda x.t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx\llbracket\lambda x.(t[q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x)])\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket\Pi(x:S).T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}))

∎

Lemma 10.36.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\quad\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vDash s:\Pi(x:S).T\quad\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vDash t:S}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash s\ t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx(s[\overrightarrow{\sigma}])\ (t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]):T[\overrightarrow{\sigma},t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]/x]}

Proof.

Apply the logical relation of
Pi
.
∎

10.3.6.
Natural Numbers

Lemma 10.37.

\frac{\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\texttt{Nat}\approx\texttt{Nat}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}}\par\frac{\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash{\textsf{zero}}\approx\textsf{zero}:\texttt{Nat}}\par\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{Nat}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\textsf{succ }t\approx\textsf{succ }t^{\prime}:\texttt{Nat}}

Proof.

Immediate.
∎

We need the following helper lemma to reason about the partial
rec
function:

Lemma 10.38.

If

•

\overrightarrow{\rho}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\rrbracket
,

•

\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:\texttt{Nat})\vDash M\approx M^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}
,

•

\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash s\approx s^{\prime}:M[\overrightarrow{I},\textsf{zero}/x]
,

•

\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:\texttt{Nat},y:M)\vDash u\approx u^{\prime}:M[\textsf{wk}_{x}\circ\textsf{wk}_{y},\textsf{succ }x/x]
and,

•

a\approx a^{\prime}\in Nat
,

then

\textsf{rec}\cdot(x.M,\llbracket s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}),(x,y.u),a,\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx\textsf{rec}\cdot(x.M^{\prime},\llbracket s^{\prime}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}),(x,y.u^{\prime}),a^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket M\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x,a)))

Proof.

We do induction on
a\approx a^{\prime}\in Nat
.

•

a=a^{\prime}=\texttt{ze}
,

\frac{}{\texttt{ze}\approx\texttt{ze}\in Nat}

then

\displaystyle\textsf{rec}\cdot(x.M\lhd\overrightarrow{\rho},\llbracket s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}),(x,y.u\lhd\overrightarrow{\rho}),\texttt{ze})

\displaystyle=\llbracket s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})

\displaystyle\approx\llbracket s^{\prime}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\textsf{rec}\cdot(x.M^{\prime}\lhd\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime},\llbracket s^{\prime}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}),(x,y.u^{\prime}\lhd\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}),\texttt{ze})

Assuming
\kappa
, we have

\displaystyle\ \textsf{rec}\cdot((x.M\lhd\overrightarrow{\rho}),\llbracket s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}),(x,y.u\lhd\overrightarrow{\rho}),\texttt{ze})[\kappa]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \llbracket s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})[\kappa]

(by naturality equality)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \llbracket s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa])

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\ \textsf{rec}\cdot((x.M\lhd\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa]),\llbracket s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa]),(x,y.u\lhd\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa]),\texttt{ze}[\kappa])

The other equality is proved symmetrically.

•

a=\texttt{su}(b)
and
a^{\prime}=\texttt{su}(b^{\prime})
,

\frac{b\approx b^{\prime}\in Nat}{\texttt{su}(b)\approx\texttt{su}(b^{\prime})\in Nat}

By IH, we have

\textsf{rec}\cdot(x.M\lhd\overrightarrow{\rho},\llbracket s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}),(x,y.u\lhd\overrightarrow{\rho}),b)\approx\textsf{rec}\cdot(x.M^{\prime}\lhd\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime},\llbracket s^{\prime}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}),(x,y.u^{\prime}\lhd\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}),b^{\prime})\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket M\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x,b)))
.
We let

\displaystyle r_{1}

\displaystyle:=\textsf{rec}\cdot(x.M\lhd\overrightarrow{\rho},\llbracket s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}),(x,y.u\lhd\overrightarrow{\rho}),b)

\displaystyle r_{2}

\displaystyle:=\textsf{rec}\cdot(x.M^{\prime}\lhd\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime},\llbracket s^{\prime}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}),(x,y.u^{\prime}\lhd\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}),b^{\prime})

Due to

\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:\texttt{Nat},y:M)\vDash u\approx u^{\prime}:M[\textsf{wk}_{x}\circ\textsf{wk}_{y},\textsf{succ }x/x]
,
we further have

\displaystyle\llbracket u\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x,b,y,r_{1}))\approx\llbracket u^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x,b^{\prime},y,r_{2}))\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket M\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x,\texttt{su}(b))))

Notice that this is our first goal.

We further assume
\kappa
. The equalities can be easily established by IH.

•

a=\uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(c)
and
a^{\prime}=\uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(c^{\prime})
,

\frac{c\approx c^{\prime}\in\bot}{\uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(c)\approx\uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(c^{\prime})\in Nat}

The first goal is established by first showing the desired values are related by

\bot
, and then realizability shows they are also related in the target PER. Assuming
\alpha
,
\kappa
,

(for some
z
)

\displaystyle\ \uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(z)\approx\uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(z)\in Nat

(for some
z^{\prime}
)

\displaystyle\ \uparrow^{\llbracket M\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,\uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(z)))}(z^{\prime})\approx\uparrow^{\llbracket M^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}[\kappa],x,\uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(z)))}(z^{\prime})\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket M\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,\uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(z))))

\displaystyle\ \llbracket M\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,\uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(z)))\approx\llbracket M^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}[\kappa],x,\uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(z)))\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

(by realizability)

\displaystyle\ \downarrow^{\textsf{U}_{i}}(\llbracket M\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,\uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(z))))\approx\downarrow^{\textsf{U}_{i}}(\llbracket M^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}[\kappa],x,\uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(z))))\in\top

and similar for
u
and
u^{\prime}
. Since all components have equal normal forms, by
applying realizability, we get the conclusion.

The equalities hold by definition.

∎

Notice that
Lemma

9.3
is proved in this helper lemma given terms are
well-typed and existing setup in the logical relations is sufficient to establish this
fact.

Rules related to induction on natural numbers are consequence of this helper lemma.
this

Lemma 10.39.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:\texttt{Nat})\vDash M\approx M^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash s\approx s^{\prime}:M[\overrightarrow{I},\textsf{zero}/x]\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:\texttt{Nat},y:M)\vDash u\approx u^{\prime}:M[\textsf{wk}_{x}\circ\textsf{wk}_{y},\textsf{succ }x/x]\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash t\approx t^{\prime}:\texttt{Nat}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash\textsf{elim}_{\texttt{Nat}}\ (x.M)\ s\ (x,y.u)\ t\approx\textsf{elim}_{\texttt{Nat}}\ (x.M^{\prime})\ s^{\prime}\ (x,y.u^{\prime})\ t^{\prime}:M[\overrightarrow{I},t/x]}

Proof.

Immediate by
Lemma

10.38
.

Lemma 10.40.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:\texttt{Nat})\vDash M:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash s:M[\overrightarrow{I},\textsf{zero}/x]\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:\texttt{Nat},y:M)\vDash u:M[\textsf{wk}_{x}\circ\textsf{wk}_{y},\textsf{succ }x/x]}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash\textsf{elim}_{\texttt{Nat}}\ (x.M)\ s\ (x,y.u)\ \textsf{zero}\approx s:M[\overrightarrow{I},\textsf{zero}/x]}

Lemma 10.41.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:\texttt{Nat})\vDash M:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash s:M[\overrightarrow{I},\textsf{zero}/x]\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:\texttt{Nat},y:M)\vDash u:M[\textsf{wk}_{x}\circ\textsf{wk}_{y},\textsf{succ }x/x]\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash t:\texttt{Nat}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vDash\textsf{elim}_{\texttt{Nat}}\ (x.M)\ s\ (x,y.u)\ (\textsf{succ }t)\approx u[\overrightarrow{I},t/x,\textsf{elim}_{\texttt{Nat}}\ (x.M)\ s\ (x,y.u)\ t/y]:M[\overrightarrow{I},\textsf{succ }t/x]}

Lemma 10.42.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\texttt{Nat}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\texttt{Nat}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}}\par\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\textsf{zero}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\textsf{zero}:\texttt{Nat}}\par\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\quad\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vDash t:\texttt{Nat}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\textsf{succ }t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\textsf{succ }(t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]):\texttt{Nat}}

The following additional lemma is need to prove the semantic substitution lemma for

Lemma 10.43.

If

•

\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta
,

•

\overrightarrow{\Delta};(\Delta,x:\texttt{Nat})\vDash M:\texttt{Ty}_{i}
,

,

\textsf{rec}\cdot(x.M,\llbracket s\rrbracket(\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})),(x,y.u),a,\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}))\approx\textsf{rec}\cdot(x.M[q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x)],\llbracket s[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}),(x,y.u[q(q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x),y)]),a,\overrightarrow{\rho})\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket M\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}),x,a)))
.

Lemma 10.44.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta\quad\overrightarrow{\Delta};(\Delta,x:\texttt{Nat})\vDash M:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\quad\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta\vDash s:M[\overrightarrow{I},\textsf{zero}/x]\quad\overrightarrow{\Delta};(\Delta,x:\texttt{Nat},y:M)\vDash u:M[\textsf{wk}_{x}\circ\textsf{wk}_{y},\textsf{succ }x/x]\quad\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta\vDash t:\texttt{Nat}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\textsf{elim}_{\texttt{Nat}}\ (x.M)\ s\ (x,y.u)\ t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\textsf{elim}_{\texttt{Nat}}\ (x.M[q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x)])\ (s[\overrightarrow{\sigma}])\ (x,y.u[q(q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x),y)])\ (t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]):M[\overrightarrow{\sigma},t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]/x]}

and

.

10.43

is needed to relate the results of elimination of natural numbers on both sides.

10.3.8.
Modality

Lemma 10.46.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\cdot\vDash T\approx T^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\square T\approx\square T^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}}

Proof.

Immediate by
Lemma
