.
∎

Lemma 10.47.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\cdot\vDash t\approx t^{\prime}:T}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\texttt{box}\ t\approx\texttt{box}\ t^{\prime}:\square T}

Proof.

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{1}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma};\cdot\vDash t\approx t^{\prime}:T

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{2}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\rho}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\rrbracket

(by definition)

\displaystyle\ \texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\cdot\rrbracket

(by
H_{1}
)

\displaystyle\ \llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}))\approx\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}))\in\mathcal{U}

(by
Lemma

10.8
)

\displaystyle\ \forall\kappa.\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}))[\kappa]\approx\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}))[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}

(first goal, by definition)

\displaystyle\ \llbracket\square T\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx\llbracket\square T\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\in\mathcal{U}

(by
H_{1}
)

\displaystyle H_{3}:

\displaystyle\ \llbracket t\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}))\approx\llbracket t^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}))\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho})))

\displaystyle\ \text{assume }\kappa,n,

(by applying
Lemma

10.8
to
H_{3}
)

\displaystyle\ \llbracket t\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}))[\kappa;\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}]\approx\llbracket t^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}))[\kappa;\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}]\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}))[\kappa;\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}])

(by abstraction)

\displaystyle\ \llbracket\texttt{box}\ t\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx\llbracket\texttt{box}\ t^{\prime}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket\square T\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}))

∎

Lemma 10.48.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash t\approx t^{\prime}:\square T\quad\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\overrightarrow{\Delta}\quad|\overrightarrow{\Delta}|=n}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vDash\texttt{unbox}_{n}\ t\approx\texttt{unbox}_{n}\ t^{\prime}:T[\overrightarrow{I};\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}]}

Proof.

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{1}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash t\approx t^{\prime}:\square T

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{2}:

\displaystyle\ \vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\overrightarrow{\Delta}

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{3}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\rho}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\overrightarrow{\Delta}\rrbracket

(by
H_{3}
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\rho}\mid{n}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\mid{n}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\rrbracket

(by
H_{1}
)

\displaystyle H_{4}:

\displaystyle\ \llbracket t\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}\mid{n})\approx\llbracket t^{\prime}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\mid{n})\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket\square T\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}\mid{n}))

(by
H_{4}
)

\displaystyle\ \texttt{unbox}\cdot(\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\rho}},{n}),\llbracket t\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}\mid{n}))\approx\texttt{unbox}\cdot(\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}},{n}),\llbracket t\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\mid{n}))\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}\mid{n},\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\rho}},{n}))))

(by abstraction)

\displaystyle\ \llbracket\texttt{unbox}_{n}\ t\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx\llbracket\texttt{unbox}_{n}\ t^{\prime}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket\square T\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}))

∎

Lemma 10.49.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\cdot\vDash t:T\quad\vdash\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\overrightarrow{\Delta}\quad|\overrightarrow{\Delta}|=n}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vDash\texttt{unbox}_{n}\ (\texttt{box}\ t)\approx t[\overrightarrow{I};\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}]:T[\overrightarrow{I};\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}]}\par\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash t:\square T}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash t\approx\texttt{box}\ (\texttt{unbox}_{1}\ t):\square T}

Proof.

Evaluate both sides and the results only differ by evaluation environments, which
can be related by definition.
∎

Lemma 10.50.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Delta};\cdot\vDash T:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\square T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\square(T[\overrightarrow{\sigma};\Uparrow^{1}\!\!{}]):\texttt{Ty}_{i}}\par\frac{\overrightarrow{\Delta};\cdot\vDash t:T\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\texttt{box}\ t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\texttt{box}\ (t[\overrightarrow{\sigma};\Uparrow^{1}\!\!{}]):\square T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]}

Proof.

Evaluate both sides. We match the evaluation environment and the goal is immediate.
∎

Lemma 10.51.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vDash t:\square T\quad|\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}|=n\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\texttt{unbox}_{n}\ t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\texttt{unbox}_{\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\sigma}},{n})}\ (t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}\mid{n}]):T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}\mid{n};\Uparrow^{\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\sigma}},{n})}\!\!{}]}

Proof.

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{1}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vDash t:\square T

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{2}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{3}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\rho}\approx\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\rrbracket

(by
H_{2}
)

\displaystyle\ \llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime})\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Delta};\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\rrbracket

(by
Lemmas

10.6
and
10.7
)

\displaystyle H_{4}:

\displaystyle\ \llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\mid{n}\approx\llbracket\overrightarrow{\sigma}\mid{n}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime}\mid{\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\sigma}},{n})})\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Delta}\rrbracket

We conclude the goal by applying
H_{1}
to
H_{4}
.
∎

10.4.
Fundamental Theorem

We have proved all semantic rules. We can thus conclude the fundamental theorem.

Theorem 10.52.

(fundamental)

•

If
\vdash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}
, then
\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}
.

•

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash t:T
.

•

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}
.

•

If
\vdash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\approx\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}
, then
\vDash\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\approx\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}
.

•

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t\approx t^{\prime}:T
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash t\approx t^{\prime}:T
.

•

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}
.

We also can show the initial environment is reflexive in
\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\rrbracket
.

Lemma 10.53.

\uparrow^{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}}\approx\uparrow^{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\rrbracket

Proof.

Immediate by
Lemma

10.17
and realizability.
∎

Theorem 10.54.

(completeness)
If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t\approx t^{\prime}:T
, then
\textsf{nbe}^{T}_{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}}(t)=\textsf{nbe}^{T}_{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}}(t^{\prime})
.

Proof.

Combine fundamental theorem and realizability.
∎

11.
Soundness for Dependent Types

11.1.
Gluing Model

Following the simply typed case, when constructing a gluing model, it has to be Kripke
w.r.t. restricted weakenings. We continue to use the definition of restricted
weakenings in
Section

6.8
and inherit its properties, as these properties do
not rely on whether types depend on terms or not.
To define the gluing model, we shall work with the values considered as
types. Luckily,
\mathcal{U}_{i}
does exactly that. The following relations are defined by
induction on
A\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
.

Definition 11.1.

Given
A\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
,

•

\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;A
denotes that
T
is a well-formed type in
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}
and
corresponds to a domain value
A
as a semantic type.

•

\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(A)
denotes that
t
has type
T
in
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}
and corresponds
to a domain value
a
which has semantic type
A
.

They are defined as follows by induction on well-foundedness of
i
and
A\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
:

•

A=\uparrow^{A^{\prime}}(C)
,

\frac{C\approx C\in\bot}{\uparrow^{A^{\prime}}(C)\approx\uparrow^{A^{\prime}}(C)\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;\mathcal{D}

\displaystyle:=\Gamma\vdash T:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\text{ and }\forall\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}.\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(C[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]):\texttt{Ty}_{i}

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime}}(c)\in El_{i}(\mathcal{D})

\displaystyle:=c\in\bot\text{ and }\Gamma\vdash T:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\text{ and }\Gamma\vdash t:T

\displaystyle\text{ and }\forall\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}.\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(C[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]):\texttt{Ty}_{i}\text{ and }

\displaystyle\qquad\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(c[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]):T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]

•

A=\textsf{N}
,

\frac{}{\textsf{N}\approx\textsf{N}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;\mathcal{D}

\displaystyle:=\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\approx\texttt{Nat}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(\mathcal{D})

\displaystyle:=\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;a\in Nat\text{ and }\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\approx\texttt{Nat}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}

where we need an auxiliary relation to relate syntactic terms of type
Nat
and
semantic values of PER
Nat
:

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t\approx\textsf{zero}:\texttt{Nat}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;\texttt{ze}\in Nat}\par\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t\approx\textsf{succ }t^{\prime}:\texttt{Nat}\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t^{\prime}:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;b\in Nat}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;\texttt{su}(b)\in Nat}\par\frac{c\in\bot\quad\forall\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}.\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(c[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]):\texttt{Nat}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;\uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(c)\in Nat}

•

A=\textsf{U}_{j}
,

\frac{j<i}{\textsf{U}_{j}\approx\textsf{U}_{j}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;\mathcal{D}

\displaystyle:=\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\approx\texttt{Ty}_{j}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(\mathcal{D})

\displaystyle:=\Gamma\vdash t:T\text{ and }\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\approx\texttt{Ty}_{j}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\text{ and }a\in\mathcal{U}_{j}\text{ and }\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t\;\circledR_{j}\;a

•

A=\blacksquare A^{\prime}
,

\frac{\forall\kappa.A^{\prime}[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}{\blacksquare A^{\prime}\approx\blacksquare A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;\blacksquare A^{\prime}

\displaystyle:=\exists T^{\prime}.\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\approx\square T^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}

\displaystyle\quad\text{ and }\forall\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime},\vdash\overrightarrow{\Delta};\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}.\ \overrightarrow{\Delta};\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\vdash T^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma};\Uparrow^{|\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}|}\!\!{}]\;\circledR_{i}\;A^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma};\Uparrow^{|\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}|}\!\!{}]

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(\blacksquare A^{\prime})

\displaystyle:=\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\text{ and }a\in\textbf{El}_{i}(\blacksquare A^{\prime})\text{ and }\exists T^{\prime}.\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\approx\square T^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\text{ and }

\displaystyle\qquad\forall\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime},\vdash\overrightarrow{\Delta};\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}.

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\overrightarrow{\Delta};\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\vdash\texttt{unbox}_{|\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}|}\ (t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]):T^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma};\Uparrow^{|\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}|}\!\!{}]\;\circledR_{i}\;\texttt{unbox}\cdot(|\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}|,a[\overrightarrow{\sigma}])\in El_{i}(A^{\prime}[\texttt{mt}(\overrightarrow{\sigma});\Uparrow^{|\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}|}\!\!{}])

•

A=Pi(A^{\prime},x.T^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho})
,

\frac{\forall\kappa.A^{\prime}[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}\quad\forall\kappa,a\approx a^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime}[\kappa]).\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,a))\approx\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}{Pi(A^{\prime},x.T^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx Pi(A^{\prime},x.T^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;Pi(A^{\prime},x.T^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho})

\displaystyle:=\exists T_{1},T_{2}.\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vdash T\approx\Pi(x:T_{1}).T_{2}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\text{ and }\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma,x:T_{1}\vdash T_{2}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\text{ and }

\displaystyle\qquad\forall\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma.\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash T_{1}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\;\circledR_{i}\;A^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\text{ and }

\displaystyle\qquad\forall\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash s:T_{1}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\;\circledR_{i}\;b\in El_{i}(A^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]).\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash T_{2}[\overrightarrow{\sigma},s/x]\;\circledR_{i}\;\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}],x,b))

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(Pi(A^{\prime},x.T^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}))

\displaystyle:=\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vdash t:T\text{ and }a\in\textbf{El}_{i}(Pi(A^{\prime},x.T^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}))

\displaystyle\text{ and }\exists T_{1},T_{2}.\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\vdash T\approx\Pi(x:T_{1}).T_{2}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\text{ and }

\displaystyle\qquad\forall\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}.\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash T_{1}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\;\circledR_{i}\;A^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\text{ and }

\displaystyle\qquad\forall\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash s:T_{1}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\;\circledR_{i}\;b\in El_{i}(A^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]).

\displaystyle\qquad\quad\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\ s:T_{2}[\overrightarrow{\sigma},s/x]\;\circledR_{i}\;a[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\cdot b\in El_{i}(\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}],x,b)))

11.2.
Properties of Gluing Model

In the following lemmas, we assume
A\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
whenever necessary.

Lemma 11.2.

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;A
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T:\texttt{Ty}_{i}
.

Lemma 11.3.

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(A)
, then

•

\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T
,

•

a\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A)
;

•

\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;A
.

Proof.

Induction on
A\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
immediately discharges most cases in the first two
statements, except

Case

A=\textsf{N}
,

\frac{}{\textsf{N}\approx\textsf{N}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

(by assumption)

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;a\in Nat

We do induction on this structure and obtain have
a\in Nat
.

In the last statement, we consider two interesting cases:

Case

A=\blacksquare A^{\prime}
,

\frac{\forall\kappa.A^{\prime}[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}{\blacksquare A^{\prime}\approx\blacksquare A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

(by assumption)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\approx\square T^{\prime}

(by assumption)

\displaystyle\ \forall\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime},\vdash\overrightarrow{\Delta};\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}.\overrightarrow{\Delta};\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\vdash\texttt{unbox}_{|\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}|}\ (t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]):T^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma};\Uparrow^{|\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}|}\!\!{}]\;\circledR_{i}\;\texttt{unbox}\cdot(|\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}|,a[\overrightarrow{\sigma}])\in El_{i}(A^{\prime}[\texttt{mt}(\overrightarrow{\sigma});\Uparrow^{|\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}|}\!\!{}])

(by IH)

\displaystyle\ \forall\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime},\vdash\overrightarrow{\Delta};\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}.\overrightarrow{\Delta};\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\vdash T^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma};\Uparrow^{|\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}|}\!\!{}]\;\circledR_{i}\;A^{\prime}[\texttt{mt}(\overrightarrow{\sigma});\Uparrow^{|\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}|}\!\!{}]

Case

A=Pi(A^{\prime},x.T^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho})
,

\frac{\forall\kappa.A^{\prime}[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}\quad\forall\kappa,a\approx a^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime}[\kappa]).\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,a))\approx\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}{Pi(A^{\prime},x.T^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx Pi(A^{\prime},x.T^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

Similar to the previous case, we just need to apply IH appropriately.

∎

Lemma 11.4 (Type conversion).

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;A
and
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\approx T^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T^{\prime}\;\circledR_{i}\;A
.

Proof.

Induction on
i
and then induction on
A\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
and apply congruence.
∎

Lemma 11.5 (Type conversion).

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(A)
and
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\approx T^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T^{\prime}\;\circledR_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(A)
.

Proof.

Induction on
i
and then induction on
A\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
, and apply congruence and conversion.
∎

Lemma 11.6 (Term conversion).

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(A)
and
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t\approx t^{\prime}:T
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t^{\prime}:T\;\circledR_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(A)
.

Proof.

Induction on
A\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
, and apply
Lemma

11.4
, congruence, conversion
and transitivity. We consider one interesting case:
A=Pi(A^{\prime},x.T^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho})
,

\frac{\forall\kappa.A^{\prime}[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}\quad\forall\kappa,a\approx a^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime}[\kappa]).\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,a))\approx\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}{Pi(A^{\prime},x.T^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx Pi(A^{\prime},x.T^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

(by assumption, for some
T_{1}
and
T_{2}
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\approx\Pi(x:T_{1}).T_{2}

\displaystyle\ \text{assume }\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma},\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash s:S[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\;\circledR_{i}\;b\in El_{i}(A^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}])

(by assumption)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\ s:T_{2}[\overrightarrow{\sigma},s/x]\;\circledR_{i}\;a[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\cdot b\in El_{i}(\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}],x,b))

\frac{\frac{\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t\approx t^{\prime}:T}{\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx t^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]:T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]}}{\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx t^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]:\Pi(x:T_{1}).T_{2}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]}\quad\frac{\frac{\lx@cref{creftypecap\lx@tilde refnum}{lem:dt:inv-glu-tm}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash s:T_{1}}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash s\approx s:T_{1}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]}}{\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\ s\approx t^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\ s:T_{2}[\overrightarrow{\sigma},s/x]}

Now IH applies.
∎

The gluing relations respect equivalence in semantic types:

Lemma 11.7 (
\mathcal{U}
conversion).

Given
A\approx A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
,

•

\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;A
and
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;A^{\prime}
are equivalent;

•

\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(A)
and
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(A^{\prime})
are equivalent.

Proof.

Induction on
A\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
:

Case

A=\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime}}(C)
,
A^{\prime}=\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime\prime}}(C^{\prime})
,

\frac{C\approx C^{\prime}\in\bot}{\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime}}(C)\approx\uparrow^{A^{\prime\prime\prime}}(C^{\prime})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

Notice that the premise implies
\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(C[\kappa])=\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(C^{\prime}[\kappa])
for
all
\alpha
and
\kappa
. We simply substitute
\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(C[\overrightarrow{\sigma}])
with

\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(C^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}])
and achieve the goal.

Case

A=\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime}
,
A^{\prime}=\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime\prime}
,

\frac{\forall\kappa.A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}{\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime}\approx\blacksquare A^{\prime\prime\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

We can resort to IHs and
Lemma

10.4
for the goal.

Case

A=Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime})
,
A^{\prime}=Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime})
,

\frac{\forall\kappa.A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}\quad\forall\kappa,a\approx a^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime\prime}[\kappa]).\llbracket T^{\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,a))\approx\llbracket T^{\prime\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime\prime\prime})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}{Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime})\approx Pi(A^{\prime\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

Assuming
\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}
,

\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash s:S[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\;\circledR_{i}\;b\in El_{i}(A^{\prime\prime\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}])
,

(by assumption, for some
T_{1}
and
T_{2}
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\approx\Pi(x:T_{1}).T_{2}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}

(by IH)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash s:T_{1}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\;\circledR_{i}\;b\in El_{i}(A^{\prime\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}])

(by
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime})
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash T_{2}[\overrightarrow{\sigma},s/x]\;\circledR_{i}\;\llbracket T^{\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}],x,b))

(by IH)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash T_{2}[\overrightarrow{\sigma},s/x]\;\circledR_{i}\;\llbracket T^{\prime\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}],x,b))

(by
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(Pi(A^{\prime\prime},x.T^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}))
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\ s:T_{2}[\overrightarrow{\sigma},s/x]\;\circledR_{i}\;a[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\cdot b\in El_{i}(\llbracket T^{\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}],x,b)))

(by IH)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\ s:T_{2}[\overrightarrow{\sigma},s/x]\;\circledR_{i}\;a[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\cdot b\in El_{i}(\llbracket T^{\prime\prime\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}^{\prime\prime\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}],x,b)))

We obtain the goals by abstraction.

∎

Lemma 11.8 (Monotonicity).

•

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;A
and
\overrightarrow{\delta}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}
, then

\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash T[\overrightarrow{\delta}]\;\circledR_{i}\;A[\overrightarrow{\delta}]
.

•

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(A)
and
\overrightarrow{\delta}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}
, then

\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\delta}]:T[\overrightarrow{\delta}]\;\circledR_{i}\;a[\overrightarrow{\delta}]\in El_{i}(A[\overrightarrow{\delta}])
.

Proof.

We do well-foundned induction on
i
and then induction on
A\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
, apply

Lemmas

11.5
and
11.6
when necessary. We
consider one case:
A=Pi(A^{\prime},x.T^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho})
,

\frac{\forall\kappa.A^{\prime}[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}\quad\forall\kappa,a\approx a^{\prime}\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A^{\prime}[\kappa]).\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,a))\approx\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\kappa],x,a^{\prime})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}{Pi(A^{\prime},x.T^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho})\approx Pi(A^{\prime},x.T^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho})\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

We know

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\approx\Pi(x:T_{1}).T_{2}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}

for some
T_{1}
and
T_{2}
. Thus we have

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash T[\overrightarrow{\delta}]\approx\Pi(x:T_{1}[\overrightarrow{\delta}]).(T_{2}[q(\overrightarrow{\delta},x)]):\texttt{Ty}_{i}

Hence we can discharge the first statement.

For the second one,
Lemma

10.8
ensures

a[\overrightarrow{\delta}]\in\textbf{El}_{i}(Pi(A^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\delta}],x.T^{\prime},\overrightarrow{\rho}[\overrightarrow{\delta}]))
. Assuming

\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Delta}
and

\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\vdash s:S[\overrightarrow{\delta}][\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\;\circledR_{i}\;b\in El_{i}(A^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\delta}][\overrightarrow{\sigma}])
, we have

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\vdash s:S[\overrightarrow{\delta}\circ\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\;\circledR_{i}\;b\in El_{i}(A^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\delta}\circ\overrightarrow{\sigma}])

and thus by premise, we have

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\delta}\circ\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\ s:T_{2}[\overrightarrow{\delta}\circ\overrightarrow{\sigma},s/x]\;\circledR_{i}\;a[\overrightarrow{\delta}\circ\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\cdot b\in El_{i}(\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\overrightarrow{\delta}\circ\overrightarrow{\sigma}],x,b)))

This is equivalent to

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\delta}][\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\ s:T_{2}[q(\overrightarrow{\delta},x)][\overrightarrow{\sigma},s/x]\;\circledR_{i}\;a[\overrightarrow{\delta}][\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\cdot b\in El_{i}(\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\overrightarrow{\delta}][\overrightarrow{\sigma}],x,b)))

and we conclude the second statement.
∎

11.3.
Realizability

Definition 11.9.

Given
A\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
,

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\overline{\circledR}_{i}\;A

\displaystyle:=\Gamma\vdash T:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\text{ and }A\in\top\text{ and }\forall\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}.\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ty}}_{\alpha}(A[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]):\texttt{Ty}_{i}

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\overline{\circledR}_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(A)

\displaystyle:=\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\text{ and }\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;A\text{ and }\downarrow^{A}(a)\in\top

\displaystyle\qquad\text{ and }\forall\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}.\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(\downarrow^{A}(a)[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]):T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\underline{\circledR}_{i}\;c\in El_{i}(A)

\displaystyle:=\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\text{ and }\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;A\text{ and }c\in\bot

\displaystyle\qquad\text{ and }\forall\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}.\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(c[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]):T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]

Lemma 11.10.

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;A
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:T)\vdash x:T[\textsf{wk}_{x}]\;\underline{\circledR}_{i}\;\alpha^{-1}(x)\in El_{i}(A)
.

Proof.

Assume
\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:T)
and then induction on it.
We know
\overrightarrow{\sigma}\approx\textsf{wk}_{x_{1}}\circ\cdots\circ\textsf{wk}_{x_{m}}
for some

m
. That means
\overrightarrow{\sigma}
is just some local weakening. Then our goal

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash x[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\alpha(\alpha^{-1}(x)):T[\textsf{wk}_{x}\circ\overrightarrow{\sigma}]

is immediate by reflexivity.

At last, we show
\alpha^{-1}(x)\in\textbf{El}_{i}(A)
by
Lemma

10.17
and
realizability of the PER model.
∎

Theorem 11.11 (Realizability).

Given
A\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
,

•

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\underline{\circledR}_{i}\;c\in El_{i}(A)
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;\uparrow^{A}(c)\in El_{i}(A)
.

•

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(A)
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\overline{\circledR}_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(A)
.

•

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;A
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\overline{\circledR}_{i}\;A
.

Proof.

We do well-foundned induction on
i
and then induction on
A\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
.

Case

A=\uparrow^{A^{\prime}}(C)
,

\frac{C\approx C\in\bot}{\uparrow^{A^{\prime}}(C)\approx\uparrow^{A^{\prime}}(C)\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

All statements are immediate by definitions.

Case

A=\textsf{N}
,

\frac{}{\textsf{N}\approx\textsf{N}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

We shall prove the second statement. We do induction on
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;a\in Nat
. Given

\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}
, we should prove

\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(\downarrow^{\textsf{N}}(a)[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]):\texttt{Nat}
.

Subcase

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t\approx\textsf{zero}:\texttt{Nat}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;\texttt{ze}\in Nat}

Immediate.

Subcase

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t\approx\textsf{succ }t^{\prime}:\texttt{Nat}\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t^{\prime}:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;b\in Nat}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;\texttt{su}(b)\in Nat}

(by IH)

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(\downarrow^{\textsf{N}}(b)[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]):\texttt{Nat}

(by congruence)

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Nf}}_{\alpha}(\downarrow^{\textsf{N}}(a)[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]):\texttt{Nat}

Subcase

\frac{c\in\bot\quad\forall\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}.\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(c[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]):\texttt{Nat}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;\uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(c)\in Nat}

Immediate by definition.

Case

A=\textsf{U}_{j}
,

\frac{j<i}{\textsf{U}_{j}\approx\textsf{U}_{j}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

To prove that if
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\underline{\circledR}_{i}\;c\in El_{i}(\textsf{U}_{j})
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;\uparrow^{A}(c)\in El_{i}(\textsf{U}_{j})
, notice that the premise gives

\displaystyle\forall\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}.\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(c[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]):\texttt{Ty}_{j}

This proposition, when given
\overrightarrow{I}:\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}
, is just
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t\;\circledR_{j}\;\uparrow^{A}(c)
and is our
goal.

Case

A=\blacksquare A^{\prime}
,

\frac{\forall\kappa.A^{\prime}[\kappa]\approx A^{\prime}[\kappa]\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}{\blacksquare A^{\prime}\approx\blacksquare A^{\prime}\in\mathcal{U}_{i}}

–

(by assumption)

From realizability, we see that the two syntactic types glued by the same value are
equivalent.

Lemma 11.12.

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;A
and
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T^{\prime}\;\circledR_{i}\;A
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\approx T^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}
.

Proof.

By realizability, we have

\displaystyle T\approx T[\overrightarrow{I}]\approx\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ty}}_{\alpha}(A)\approx T^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{I}]\approx T^{\prime}

∎

11.4.
Cumulativity

Similar to the completeness case (
Lemma

10.9
), when proving cumulativity,
we also need to mutually prove a lowering theorem. This again is because in the
dependent function case, the input is in a contravariant position. When we lift a
level in the output, we are only given an input on a higher level. Unlike in the
completeness case, where cumulativity and lowering are rather uniform, in the gluing
model, lowering does not come for free: since the gluing model also stores syntactic
information, we must have enough syntactic information in order to prove
lowering. More precisely, we prove cumulativity as follows:

Lemma 11.13 (Cumulativity and lowering).

Given
A\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
,

•

if
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;A
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{1+i}\;A
;

•

if
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(A)
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{1+i}\;a\in El_{1+i}(A)
;

•

if
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{1+i}\;a\in El_{1+i}(A)
and
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;A
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(A)
.

The first two statements are intuitive cumulativity. The last one is lowering. Notice
that in order to lower
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{1+i}\;a\in El_{1+i}(A)
by one level, we must know that
T

and
A
are related one level lower (i.e. the premise
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;A
).

Proof.

We proceed by induction on
A\in\mathcal{U}_{i}
. The trickiest cases is
Pi
. When proving
cumulativity, we must apply lowering to the argument so that the input level is
lowered by one, using which we apply IH; vice versa, we apply cumulativity when
proving lowering, where we must increase the input level by one so that we can use
it to apply IH.
∎

Once we establish one-step cumulativity, we can generalize it to any greater level:

Lemma 11.14 (Cumulativity).

If
i\leq j
and
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{i}\;A
and
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{i}\;a\in El_{i}(A)
, resp., then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{j}\;A
and

\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{j}\;a\in El_{j}(A)
, resp..

Due to cumulativity, we can do the same as the PER models, where we hide the levels by
taking limits of the gluing model. That is

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR\;A

\displaystyle:=\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash T\;\circledR_{\infty}\;A

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR\;a\in El(A)

\displaystyle:=\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR_{\infty}\;a\in El_{\infty}(A)

These limits are well defined because if
T
and
A
are related by the limit, then
cumulativity ensures that they must be related by some concrete level in natural
numbers (but we do not have to be concerned about its actual level). Similarly, if
t

and
a
are related by the limit, they are actually related at some level.

11.5.
Gluing of Substitutions

We generalize
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T\;\circledR\;a\in El(A)
to a gluing relation between substitutions and
evaluation environments.

Definition 11.15.

\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}
relates
\overrightarrow{\sigma}
and
\overrightarrow{\rho}
and is defined by
induction on
\overrightarrow{\Delta}
:

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\epsilon;\cdot\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}

\displaystyle:=\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\epsilon;\cdot

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};\cdot\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}

\displaystyle:=\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};\cdot

\displaystyle\text{ and }\exists\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime},n.\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\mid{n}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}\mid{1}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\text{ and }\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\sigma}},{1})=\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\rho}},{1})=n\text{ and }

\displaystyle\qquad\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\mid{n}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}\mid{1}

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};(\Delta,x:T)\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}

\displaystyle:=\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};(\Delta,x:T)

\displaystyle\text{ and }\exists\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime},t.\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\textsf{wk}_{x}\circ\overrightarrow{\sigma}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta\text{ and }\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash x[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx t:T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}]\text{ and }

\displaystyle\qquad\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x))\in\mathcal{U}\text{ and }

\displaystyle\qquad\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}]\;\circledR\;\rho(x)\in El(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x)))\text{ and }

(where
(\_,\rho):=\overrightarrow{\rho}(0)
)

\displaystyle\qquad\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta\;\circledR\;\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x)

Lemma 11.16.

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}
, then
\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\sigma}},{n})=\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\rho}},{n})
.

Lemma 11.17.

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\mid{\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\sigma}},{n})}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}\mid{n}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\mid{n}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}\mid{n}
.

Proof.

Immediate by induction on
n
and
\overrightarrow{\Delta}
.
∎

Lemma 11.18.

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}
.

Proof.

Immediate.
∎

Lemma 11.19.

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}
, then
\overrightarrow{\rho}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Delta}\rrbracket
.

Proof.

Notice that
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}
and thus
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vDash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}
and
\vDash\overrightarrow{\Delta}
, so
\overrightarrow{\rho}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Delta}\rrbracket
is a well-defined statement. We do induction on
\overrightarrow{\Delta}
.

Case

\overrightarrow{\Delta}=\epsilon;\cdot
or
\overrightarrow{\Delta}=\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime};\cdot
, then it is
immediate. Apply IH when necessary.

Case

\overrightarrow{\Delta}=\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime};(\Delta,x:T)
, then

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{1}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}]\;\circledR\;\rho(x)\in El(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x)))

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{2}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta\;\circledR\;\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x)

(by
H_{1}
and
Lemma

11.3
)

\displaystyle\ \rho(x)\in\textbf{El}(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x)))

(by IH)

\displaystyle\ \texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x)\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta\rrbracket

(by definition)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\rho}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Delta}\rrbracket

∎

Lemma 11.20.

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}
and
\vdash\overrightarrow{\Delta}\approx\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}
, then

\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}
.

Proof.

We do induction on
\vdash\overrightarrow{\Delta}\approx\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}
.

Case

\frac{}{\vdash\epsilon;\cdot\approx\epsilon;\cdot}

Immediate.

Case

\overrightarrow{\Delta}=\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime};\cdot
,
\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}=\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime\prime};\cdot
,

\frac{\vdash\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime}\approx\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime\prime}}{\vdash\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime};\cdot\approx\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime\prime};\cdot}

Apply conversion when necessary. Finally,

(by assumption, for some
\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}
and
n
)

\displaystyle H_{1}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\mid{n}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}\mid{1}

(by
H_{1}
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\mid{n}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}\mid{1}

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}

Case

\overrightarrow{\Delta}=\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime};(\Delta,x:T)
,
\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}=\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime\prime};(\Delta^{\prime},x:T^{\prime})
,

\frac{\vdash\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime};\Delta\approx\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime\prime};\Delta^{\prime}\quad\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime};\Delta\vdash T\approx T^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}}{\vdash\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime};(\Delta,x:T)\approx\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime\prime};(\Delta^{\prime},x:T^{\prime})}

Interestingly, we need to apply the fundamental theorem of the PER model:

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{2}:

\displaystyle\ \vdash\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime};\Delta\approx\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime\prime};\Delta^{\prime}

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{3}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime};\Delta\vdash T\approx T^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}

(by
Theorem

10.52
)

\displaystyle H_{4}:

\displaystyle\ \vDash\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime};\Delta\approx\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime\prime};\Delta^{\prime}

(by
Theorem

10.52
)

\displaystyle H_{5}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime};\Delta\vDash T\approx T^{\prime}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}

(by
Lemma

11.19
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\rho}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Delta}\rrbracket

(by
Lemma

10.13
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\rho}\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\rrbracket

(by definition)

\displaystyle\ \texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x)\in\llbracket\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime\prime};\Delta^{\prime}\rrbracket

(by
H_{3}
)

\displaystyle\ \llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x))\approx\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x))\in\mathcal{U}_{i}

(PER property)

\displaystyle\ \llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x))\in\mathcal{U}

(by
Lemma

11.7
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}]\;\circledR\;\rho(x)\in El(\llbracket T^{\prime}\rrbracket(\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x)))

(by IH)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime\prime};\Delta^{\prime}\;\circledR\;\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x)

(by definition)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}

∎

Lemma 11.21.

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}
and
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\delta}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}
, then

\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\delta}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}
.

Proof.

We do induction on
\overrightarrow{\Delta}
. In each case, we apply presupposition and properties of

L
and truncation.
∎

Lemma 11.22.

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}
and
\overrightarrow{\delta}:\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Gamma}
, then

\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}[\overrightarrow{\delta}]
.

Proof.

Immediate by induction on
\overrightarrow{\Delta}
.

Case

\overrightarrow{\Delta}=\epsilon;\cdot
, immediate.

Case

\overrightarrow{\Delta}=\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime};\cdot
, then

(by assumption, for some
\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}
and
n
)

\displaystyle H_{1}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\mid{n}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}\mid{1}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}

(by assumption)

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\sigma}},{1})=\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\rho}},{1})=n

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{2}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\mid{n}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}\mid{1}

(by
H_{1}
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}\mid{\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\delta}},{n})}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}\mid{1}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta}\mid{n}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta}\mid{n}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}\mid{\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\delta}},{n})}\vdash(\overrightarrow{\sigma}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta})\mid{1}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta}\mid{n}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}

\displaystyle\ \mathcal{O}({(\overrightarrow{\sigma}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta})},{1})=\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\delta}},{\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\sigma}},{1})})=\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\delta}},{\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\rho}},{1})})=\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\rho}[\overrightarrow{\delta}]},{1})=\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\delta}},{n})

Thus we let the existentials in the goal to be
\overrightarrow{\sigma}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta}\mid{n}
and

\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\delta}},{n})
.

(by IH)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}\mid{\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\delta}},{n})}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta}\mid{n}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}\mid{1}[\overrightarrow{\delta}\mid{n}]

(by definition)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}\mid{\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\delta}},{n})}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta}\mid{n}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}[\overrightarrow{\delta}]\mid{1}

(by abstraction)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\delta}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}

Case

\overrightarrow{\Delta}=\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime};(\Delta,x:T)
, then

\displaystyle H_{3}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\textsf{wk}_{x}\circ\overrightarrow{\sigma}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta

(by assumption, for some
\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}
and
t
)

\displaystyle H_{4}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash x[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\approx t:T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}]

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{5}:

\displaystyle\ \llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x))\in\mathcal{U}

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{6}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}]\;\circledR\;\rho(x)\in El(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x)))

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{7}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta\;\circledR\;\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x)

Notice that

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}\vdash\textsf{wk}_{x}\circ\overrightarrow{\sigma}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta}\approx\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}\vdash x[\overrightarrow{\sigma}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta}]\approx t[\overrightarrow{\delta}]:T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta}]

so we let the existentials in the goal be
\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta}
and

t[\overrightarrow{\delta}]
.

(by
Lemma

10.8
)

\displaystyle\ \llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\overrightarrow{\delta}],x))\in\mathcal{U}

(by
Lemmas

11.8
and
11.5
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\delta}]:T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta}]\;\circledR\;\rho[\overrightarrow{\delta}](x)\in El(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\overrightarrow{\delta}],x)))

(by IH)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}^{\prime}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta}:\overrightarrow{\Delta};\Delta\;\circledR\;\texttt{drop}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\overrightarrow{\delta}],x)

(by abstraction)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\delta}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}

∎

Finally, we can define the semantic judgment:

Definition 11.23.

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\Vdash t:T

\displaystyle:=\forall\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}.\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]:T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\;\circledR\;\llbracket t\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\in El(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}))

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\Vdash\overrightarrow{\delta}:\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}

\displaystyle:=\forall\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}.\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash\overrightarrow{\delta}\circ\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Gamma}^{\prime}\;\circledR\;\llbracket\overrightarrow{\delta}\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})

Lemma 11.29.

\frac{x:T\in\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\Vdash x:T}

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{2}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}

(for some

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash x[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]:T^{\prime}[\textsf{wk}_{x}][\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\;\circledR\;\rho(x)\in El(\llbracket T^{\prime}[\textsf{wk}_{x}]\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}))

Lemma 11.31.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\Vdash S:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:S)\Vdash t:T}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\Vdash\lambda x.t:\Pi(x:S).T}

11.22

H_{1}
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash T[\overrightarrow{\sigma},s[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]/x]:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\;\circledR\;\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x,\llbracket s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})))\in El(\textsf{U}_{i})

(by definition)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash T[\overrightarrow{\sigma},s[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]/x]\;\circledR\;\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x,\llbracket s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})))

(by
H_{8}
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash T^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma},s[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]/x]\;\circledR\;\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x,\llbracket s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})))

(by
Lemma

11.12
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash T[\overrightarrow{\sigma},s[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]/x]\approx T^{\prime}[\overrightarrow{\sigma},s[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]/x]

(by
H_{8}
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash(t\ s)[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]:T[\overrightarrow{\sigma},s[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]/x]\;\circledR\;\llbracket t\ s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\in El(\llbracket T[\overrightarrow{I},s/x]\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}))

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash(t\ s)[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]:T[\overrightarrow{I},s/x][\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\;\circledR\;\llbracket t\ s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\in El(\llbracket T[\overrightarrow{I},s/x]\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}))

By abstraction, this concludes the goal.
∎

11.6.2.
Natural Numbers

Lemma 11.33.

\frac{}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\Vdash\texttt{Nat}:\texttt{Ty}_{i}}\par\frac{}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\Vdash\textsf{zero}:\texttt{Nat}}\par\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\Vdash t:\texttt{Nat}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\Vdash\textsf{succ }t:\texttt{Nat}}

Proof.

Immediate.
∎

We need a lemma in order to handle recursion. It effectively keeps track of evaluation
and shows that the gluing is maintained.

Lemma 11.34.

If

•

\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}
,

•

\forall\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t^{\prime}:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;a\in El(\textsf{N}).\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash M[\overrightarrow{\sigma},t^{\prime}/x]\;\circledR\;\llbracket M\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x,a))
,

•

\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash s[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]:M[\overrightarrow{\sigma},\textsf{zero}/x]\;\circledR\;\llbracket s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\in El(\llbracket M\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x,\texttt{ze})))
,

•

\forall\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t^{\prime}:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;a\in El(\textsf{N}),\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash u^{\prime}:M[\overrightarrow{\sigma},t^{\prime}/x]\;\circledR\;b\in El(\llbracket M\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x,a))).\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash u[\overrightarrow{\sigma},t^{\prime}/x,u^{\prime}/y]:M[\overrightarrow{\sigma},\textsf{succ }t^{\prime}/x]\;\circledR\;\llbracket u\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x,a,y,b))\in El(\llbracket M\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x,\texttt{su}(a))))

•

\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;a\in Nat
,

then

\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash\textsf{elim}_{\texttt{Nat}}\ (x.M[q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x)])\ (s[\overrightarrow{\sigma}])\ (x,y.u[q(q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x),y)])\ t:M[\overrightarrow{\sigma},t/x]\;\circledR\;\textsf{rec}\cdot(x.M,\overrightarrow{\rho},\llbracket s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}),(x,y.u,\overrightarrow{\rho}),a)\in El(\llbracket M\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x,a)))
.

Proof.

We do induction on
\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;a\in Nat
.

Case

a=\texttt{ze}
,

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t\approx\textsf{zero}:\texttt{Nat}}{\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;\texttt{ze}\in Nat}

We have

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash\textsf{elim}_{\texttt{Nat}}\ (x.M[q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x)])\ (s[\overrightarrow{\sigma}])\ (x,y.u[q(q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x),y)])\ \textsf{zero}\approx s[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]:M[\overrightarrow{\sigma},\textsf{zero}/x]

so the goal is discharged by

\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash s[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]:M[\overrightarrow{\sigma},\textsf{zero}/x]\;\circledR\;\llbracket s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\in El(\llbracket M\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x,\texttt{ze})))
modulo

Lemmas

11.5
and
11.6
.

Case

a=\texttt{su}(a^{\prime})
,

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t\approx\textsf{succ }t^{\prime}:\texttt{Nat}\quad\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t^{\prime}:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;a^{\prime}\in Nat}{\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;\texttt{su}(b)\in Nat}

We have

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash\textsf{elim}_{\texttt{Nat}}\ (x.M[q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x)])\ (s[\overrightarrow{\sigma}])\ (x,y.u[q(q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x),y)])\ t\approx

\displaystyle{u[\overrightarrow{\sigma},t^{\prime}/x,\textsf{elim}_{\texttt{Nat}}\ (x.M[q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x)])\ (s[\overrightarrow{\sigma}])\ (x,y.u[q(q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x),y)])\ t^{\prime}/y]}:M[\overrightarrow{\sigma},t/x]

We first have

\displaystyle\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t^{\prime}:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;a^{\prime}\in El(\textsf{N})

Then by IH, we have

\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash\textsf{elim}_{\texttt{Nat}}\ (x.M[q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x)])\ (s[\overrightarrow{\sigma}])\ (x,y.u[q(q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x),y)])\ t^{\prime}:M[\overrightarrow{\sigma},x:t^{\prime}]\;\circledR\;\textsf{rec}\cdot(x.M,\overrightarrow{\rho},\llbracket s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}),(x,y.u,\overrightarrow{\rho}),a^{\prime})\in El(\llbracket M\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x,a^{\prime})))
. We plug this in the
u

case and then we are done modulo

Lemmas

11.5
and
11.6
.

Case

a=\uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(c)
,

\frac{c\in\bot\quad\forall\overrightarrow{\delta}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Delta}.\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\delta}]\approx\textsf{R}^{\textsf{Ne}}_{\alpha}(c[\overrightarrow{\delta}]):\texttt{Nat}}{\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;\uparrow^{\textsf{N}}(c)\in Nat}

We prove

\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash\textsf{elim}_{\texttt{Nat}}\ (x.M[q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x)])\ (s[\overrightarrow{\sigma}])\ (x,y.u[q(q(\overrightarrow{\sigma},x),y)])\ t:M[\overrightarrow{\sigma},t/x]\;\underline{\circledR}\;\textsf{rec}(x.M,\overrightarrow{\rho},\llbracket s\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}),(x,y.u,\overrightarrow{\rho}),c)\in El(\llbracket M\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho},x,a)))
instead and apply
realizability to obtain the goal.

To prove the subgoal, we assume
\overrightarrow{\delta}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Delta}
, and then prove the
subgoal by just following the definitions and realizability.
∎

Lemma 11.35.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:\texttt{Nat})\Vdash M:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\Vdash s:M[\overrightarrow{I},\textsf{zero}/x]\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma};(\Gamma,x:\texttt{Nat},y:M)\Vdash u:M[\textsf{wk}_{x}\circ\textsf{wk}_{y},\textsf{succ }x/x]\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\Vdash t:\texttt{Nat}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\Vdash\textsf{elim}_{\texttt{Nat}}\ (x.M)\ s\ (x,y.u)\ t:M[\overrightarrow{I},t/x]}

Proof.

Assuming
\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\Gamma\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}
, we extract

\overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]:\texttt{Nat}\;\circledR\;\llbracket t\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\in Nat
. Then we apply the previous lemma
and obtain the goal up to

Lemmas

11.5
and
11.6
.
∎

11.6.4.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\cdot\Vdash T:\texttt{Ty}_{i}}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\Vdash\square T:\texttt{Ty}_{i}}

Proof.

Immediate by
Lemma

11.8
.
∎

Lemma 11.38.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\cdot\Vdash t:T}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\Vdash\texttt{box}\ t:\square T}

Proof.

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{1}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma};\cdot\Vdash t:T

(by assumption)

\displaystyle H_{2}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\;\circledR\;\overrightarrow{\rho}

\displaystyle\ \text{assume }\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime},\vdash\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime};\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime},\overrightarrow{\delta}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\Rightarrow_{r}\overrightarrow{\Delta},\text{let }n=|\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime}|

(by
Lemma

11.22
)

\displaystyle H_{3}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime};\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime}\vdash(\overrightarrow{\sigma}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta});\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}:\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\cdot\;\circledR\;\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\overrightarrow{\delta}],n)

(by
H_{1}
and
H_{3}
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime};\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime}\vdash t[(\overrightarrow{\sigma}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta});\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}]:T[(\overrightarrow{\sigma}\circ\overrightarrow{\delta});\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}]\;\circledR\;\llbracket t\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\overrightarrow{\delta}],n))\in El(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}[\overrightarrow{\delta}],n)))

(by

11.6
and

)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime};\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma};\Uparrow^{1}\!\!{}][\overrightarrow{\delta};\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}]:T[\overrightarrow{\sigma};\Uparrow^{1}\!\!{}][\overrightarrow{\delta};\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}]\;\circledR\;\llbracket t\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}))[\overrightarrow{\delta};\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}]\in El(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}))[\overrightarrow{\delta};\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}])

(by
Lemma

11.6
)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime};\overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime\prime}\vdash\texttt{unbox}_{n}\ (\texttt{box}\ t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}][\overrightarrow{\delta}]):T[\overrightarrow{\sigma};\Uparrow^{1}\!\!{}][\overrightarrow{\delta};\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}]\;\circledR\;\texttt{unbox}\cdot(n,\llbracket\texttt{box}\ t\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})[\overrightarrow{\delta}])\in El(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}))[\overrightarrow{\delta};\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}])

(by abstraction)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}\vdash\texttt{box}\ t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]:\square T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\;\circledR\;\llbracket\texttt{box}\ t\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\in El(\llbracket\square T\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}))

∎

Lemma 11.39.

\frac{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\cdot\Vdash T:\texttt{Ty}_{i}\quad\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\Vdash t:\square T\quad|\overrightarrow{\Delta}|=n}{\overrightarrow{\Gamma};\overrightarrow{\Delta}\Vdash\texttt{unbox}_{n}\ t:T[\overrightarrow{I};\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}]}

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Gamma}\Vdash t:\square T

(by assumption)

11.17
)

and
H_{2}
)

\displaystyle H_{3}:

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\mid{\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\sigma}},{n})}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}\mid{n}]:\square T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}\mid{n}]\;\circledR\;\llbracket t\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}\mid{n})\in El(\llbracket\square T\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}\mid{n}))

(by

)

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\vdash\texttt{unbox}_{\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\sigma}},{n})}\ (t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}\mid{n}]):T[\overrightarrow{\sigma}\mid{n};\Uparrow^{\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\sigma}},{n})}\!\!{}]\;\circledR\;\texttt{unbox}\cdot(\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\rho}},{n}),\llbracket t\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}\mid{n}))\in El(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\texttt{ext}(\overrightarrow{\rho}\mid{n},\mathcal{O}({\overrightarrow{\rho}},{n}))))

(by definition and

and

\displaystyle\ \overrightarrow{\Delta}^{\prime}\vdash\texttt{unbox}_{n}\ t[\overrightarrow{\sigma}]:T[\overrightarrow{I};\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}][\overrightarrow{\sigma}]\;\circledR\;\llbracket\texttt{unbox}_{n}\ t\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho})\in El(\llbracket T[\overrightarrow{I};\Uparrow^{n}\!\!{}]\rrbracket(\overrightarrow{\rho}))

∎

11.7.
Fundamental Theorem

Since we have proven the semantic typing rules, we can conclude the fundamental
theorem:

Theorem 11.40 (Fundamental).

•

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\Vdash t:T
.

•

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\Vdash\overrightarrow{\sigma}:\overrightarrow{\Delta}
.

In the theorem, we need to work in a slightly adjusted system with some extra premises
for strong IHs. We can discharge these premises once the theorem is established.

We also need to relate
\overrightarrow{I}
and
\uparrow^{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}}
:

Lemma 11.41.

\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash\overrightarrow{I}:\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\;\circledR\;\uparrow^{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}}

Proof.

Immediate by induction on
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}
and apply
Lemma

11.10
.
∎

Theorem 11.42 (Soundness).

If
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t:T
, then
\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t\approx\textsf{nbe}^{T}_{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}}(t):T
.

Proof.

Applying the fundamental theorem, we have

\overrightarrow{\Gamma}\vdash t[\overrightarrow{I}]:T[\overrightarrow{I}]\;\circledR\;\llbracket t\rrbracket(\uparrow^{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}})\in El(\llbracket T\rrbracket(\uparrow^{\overrightarrow{\Gamma}}))
. We conclude the
goal by applying
Lemmas

11.5
and
11.6
and realizability.
∎
